# Change log

One row per section of the source site, from `audit/change-control.json` (1531 sections on
264 pages). Each decision was applied with `sr-plan --set` by the rule in
`src/tools/ledger-decide.mjs` that matched the section's source class; `audit/ledger-rules.json` records
which rule decided which row.

## Legend

- **PRESERVE** — kept as-is; content and function unchanged
- **IMPROVE** — same content and meaning, better structure, hierarchy or design
- **REPLACE** — superseded by a better structure (reason required)
- **REMOVE** — deliberately dropped (reason required)
- **ADD** — new section that did not exist on the source

Tally: IMPROVE 1265 · REPLACE 266. Nothing was removed as a section.

## Decisions by component

| rule | sections | decision | narrative slot | preset | why |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| announce | 264 | IMPROVE | strategic-cta | cta-banner | Announcement bar ("Thanks for visiting our new website!", links to /shop) rebuilt as one static line: auto-moving text needs a pause control (WCAG 2.2.2). Same copy and link. |
| megamenu | 264 | IMPROVE | footer | navbar | Mega menu rebuilt as a neumorphic panel + mobile drawer from the captured categories, classifications and brand logos; dead brand links remapped or dropped per src/content/link-fixes.json. |
| hero-video | 1 | IMPROVE | value-proposition | 01-transitions-hero-motion-8-the-dominant-hero | Hero video (the client's own 1920x1080 file, a Cloudinary code embed on the source) rebuilt full-bleed at 100vw with the site tagline as the H1. |
| separator | 265 | REPLACE | — | 00-top12-8-image-bottom-fade-directional-scrim-into-the-next-section | Decorative separator image replaced by the redesign's layered section edges (hero clay lip, raised/inset bands). |
| home-categories | 1 | REPLACE | value-proposition | 01-transitions-hero-motion-11-your-product-floating | Category tile artwork replaced by fal-generated product photography with pop-out cut-outs (operator request); every label and link preserved. |
| home-loyalty | 1 | IMPROVE | strategic-cta | cta-banner | Sun Club loyalty banner (client image, links to the Treez loyalty sign-up) rebuilt as a layered, tilting frame. |
| home-marquee | 1 | IMPROVE | value-proposition | 00-top12-4-scroll-reveal-entrance-with-sibling-stagger | Tagline marquee rebuilt as a tilted wall of type whose lines rise only as the page scrolls (never auto-moving, WCAG 2.2.2); the source's 12 copies kept, one broken item linking to a 404 dropped. |
| home-about | 1 | IMPROVE | trust-positioning | 01-transitions-hero-motion-20-parallax | About block rebuilt as a layered split: the client's photo, a fal canopy image and a flower cut-out breaking the frame. |
| express | 264 | IMPROVE | strategic-cta | cta-banner | Treez fulfilment banner rebuilt as the shop-page ribbon, the footer band and the fulfilment chooser, carrying both captured states. |
| home-rail | 9 | IMPROVE | strategic-cta | 14-ecommerce-3-sgen-living-styleguide-14-e-commerce-flow-components-live-3 | Product carousel / Today's Deals rebuilt as scroll-snap rails and a deals panel (card images joined from each product page). |
| banner | 93 | IMPROVE | value-proposition | 01-transitions-hero-motion-11-your-product-floating | Page banner rebuilt with display type and fal pop-out category art or the source banner in a layered frame. |
| content-split | 22 | IMPROVE | trust-positioning | 01-transitions-hero-motion-20-parallax | Section rebuilt in the sectionized split layout with layered images; copy verbatim. |
| gallery | 4 | IMPROVE | trust-positioning | 03-layout-imagery-components-5-living-styleguide-03-layout-imagery-components-sgen-5 | Strain gallery rebuilt as layered framed cards with a strain-tag row. |
| article-body | 8 | IMPROVE | benefits-solution | 00-top12-2-display-face-on-headings-oversized-tight-negative-tracked-type | Article body rebuilt as prose with display headings; copy verbatim (source copy issues listed for the client in docs). |
| post-cards | 72 | IMPROVE | benefits-solution | 00-top12-3-soft-large-blur-ink-tinted-card-shadow-lift-on-hover | Post cards rebuilt as lifted neumorphic cards; titles, dates and excerpts verbatim. |
| grid | 89 | IMPROVE | benefits-solution | 14-ecommerce-2-sgen-living-styleguide-14-e-commerce-flow-components-live-2 | Product grid rebuilt from the page's own ItemList (the live grid rendered empty) with client-side filter/sort using the source's labels. |
| catlinks | 20 | IMPROVE | strategic-cta | 10-sections-12-color-code-the-path | Category link carousel rebuilt as neumorphic chips. |
| testimonials | 3 | IMPROVE | social-proof | 00-top12-3-soft-large-blur-ink-tinted-card-shadow-lift-on-hover | Testimonials restyled as raised quote cards; every quote and name kept verbatim. Flagged in docs: the quotes name other businesses (Stiiizy, Rancho Santa Fe) and read as template copy. |
| faq | 3 | IMPROVE | objection-handling | accordion-faq | FAQ rebuilt as neumorphic accordions with real question/answer pairs. |
| product-details | 146 | IMPROVE | benefits-solution | 01-transitions-hero-motion-11-your-product-floating | Product detail rebuilt: layered gallery stage, buy box in the captured availability state, lab data as bars, Prop 65 accordion. |

Presets: every rebuilt row names one from the indexed library (`audit/preset-match.json`, 0 undecided).
The layout matcher had matched 80 rows by signature; all 80 were answered with the preset the
redesign actually built (`audit/ledger-rules.json` → matcherOverrides). 67 rows on `?sort=` pages hold an
`=` in their id, which `sr-match --answer` cannot parse; they were written with that command's own logic
(→ answeredDirect).

| <title> and description, /strain/{cbd,hybrid,indica,sativa,si} | the capitalised twin page’s title (the crawl stored both spellings in one file on a case-insensitive disk) | the lowercase page’s own H1 | the lowercase URL is canonical; its own heading is its title |
| store status (store locator, dispensary page) | "CLOSED until 10:00 AM ET" — frozen at crawl time | "OPEN" / "CLOSED" + "until …" computed in the browser from the published hours | a frozen status is wrong most of the day; "OPEN" is the only new word, the counterpart of the source’s "CLOSED" |
| announcement bar | "Thanks for visiting our new website!" in a swiper | the same line, static, linking to /shop as the source does | moving text needs a pause control (WCAG 2.2.2) |

### Structure that changed (same strings, different component)

| where | on the source | here | why |
|---|---|---|---|
| /dispensary/sunny-dayz, /weed-delivery/dispensary-in-glendale | sliders (testimonials, category tiles, city cards, product carousel); the page capture flattened each product card into loose lines and placed every slider before its heading | each slider rebuilt as its component under its own heading: quote cards, linked category tiles, city cards, and a product rail of the real product pages matched by exact name | the flattened capture rendered as ~9,700px of raw lines under the wrong headings; the one carousel product with no product page in the crawl (Doja Confetti - 3.5G Flower) is listed under Removed |
| /dispensary/sunny-dayz | store banner photo (alt "Banner location page") inside the body; the Hours list as plain text | the store photo is the page’s hero image; the hours are the two-column hours list with today highlighted | the photo was otherwise never shown; the list text ran day and time together |
| /contact-us | each reviewer name fused onto the end of its quote | the name split back out as the quote’s caption (both strings verbatim) | the contact values had been read as the reviewers’ names |
| 404 pages | GO BACK and SHOP NOW buttons (script-driven) | GO BACK returns to the previous page (home when there is none); SHOP NOW links to /shop | they rendered as inert labels; /shop is the site’s own "Shop" destination |
| /shop | empty promo carousel: "Promo Carousel", SEE ALL (0), "0 promotions available" read to screen readers only | same strings; the count stays screen-reader-only, no "0" badge | as the source did it |
| /daily-deals | breadcrumb only; the page renders nothing else | the in-page express ribbon is omitted so it does not repeat the footer’s identical call to action; no copy added | two identical CTAs back to back |

## Imagery

All photography of the client's products, brands, stores and people is the client's own, downloaded
from the live site and re-encoded to WebP (`audit/image-inventory.json`). 12 images were
**generated** (fal.ai) for the category tiles and banners, plus background-removed cut-outs of 11 of
them for the pop-out frames (`assets/generated/manifest.json`, which also records each prompt, seed and hash).

Why generating them was safe: each is a generic studio still life of a product *category* — it shows
no person, no real place, no specific branded product, no packaging text or logo (every prompt forbids
text and logos; an early vape image that rendered a glyph was regenerated), and it makes no claim
about a result. The safety checker passed all of them (`nsfw: false`). They replace only the
category artwork the source used; product photos stay the client's.

| image | category | model | depicts | cut-out |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `src/assets/img/gen/concentrates` | CONCENTRATES | fal-ai/flux-pro/v1.1-ultra | a glossy golden amber cannabis rosin concentrate on a small round clear glass dish, honey-like texture catching the light, a small stainless dab tool beside it | yes (fal-ai/birefnet) |
| `src/assets/img/gen/flower` | FLOWER | fal-ai/flux-pro/v1.1-ultra | a single large dense cannabis flower bud, frosty sparkling trichomes, amber and orange pistils, deep green and violet hues, centered and isolated | yes (fal-ai/birefnet) |
